<section class="language-reading" lang="en">
<h3>English</h3>

<p dir="ltr">Malik related to me from Humayd ibn Qays al-Makki that a son of al-Mutawakkil had a mukatab who died at Makka and left (enough to pay) the rest of his kitaba and he owed some debts to people. He also left a daughter. The governor of Makka was not certain about how to judge in the case, so he wrote to Abd al-Malik ibn Marwan to ask him about it. Abd al-Malik wrote to him, &quot;Begin with the debts owed to people, and then pay what remains of his kitaba. Then divide what remains of the property between the daughter and the master.&quot; Malik said, &quot;What is done among us is that the master of a slave does not have to give his slave a kitaba if he asks for it. I have not heard of any of the Imams forcing a man to give a kitaba to his slave. I heard that one of the people of knowledge, when someone asked about that and mentioned that Allah the Blessed, the Exalted, said, &#x27;Give them their kitaba, if you know some good in them&#x27; (Sura 24 ayat 33) recited these two ayats, &#x27;When you are free of the state of ihram, then hunt for game.&#x27; (Sura 5 ayat 3) &#x27;When the prayer is finished, scatter in the land and seek Allah&#x27;s favour.&#x27; &quot; (Sura 62 ayat 10) Malik commented, &quot;It is a way of doing things for which Allah, the Mighty, the Majestic, has given permission to people, and it is not obligatory for them.&quot; Malik said, &quot;I heard one of the people of knowledge say about the word of Allah, the Blessed, the Exalted, &#x27;Give them of the wealth which Allah has given you,&#x27; that it meant that a man give his slave a kitaba and then reduce the end of his kitaba for him by some specific amount.&quot; Malik said, &quot;This is what I have heard from the people of knowledge and what I see people doing here.&quot; Malik said, &quot;I have heard that Abdullah ibn Umar gave one of his slaves his kitaba for 35,000 dirhams, and then reduced the end of his kitaba by 5,000 dirhams.&quot; Malik said, &quot;What is done among us is that when a master gives a mukatab his kitaba, the mukatab&#x27;s property goes with him but his children do not go with him unless he stipulates that in his kitaba.&quot; Yahya said, &quot;I heard Malik say that if a mukatab whose master had given him a kitaba had a slave- girl who was pregnant by him, and neither he nor his master knew that on the day he was given his kitaba, the child did not follow him because he was not included in the kitaba. He belonged to the master. As for the slave-girl, she belonged to the mukatab because she was his property.&quot; Malik said that if a man and his wife&#x27;s son (by another husband) inherited a mukatab from the wife and the mukatab died before he had completed his kitaba, they divided his inheritance between them according to the Book of Allah. If the slave paid his kitaba and then died, his inheritance went to the son of the woman, and the husband had nothing of his inheritance. Malik said that if a mukatab gave his own slave a kitaba, the situation was looked at. If he wanted to do his slave a favour and it was obvious by his making it easy for him, that was not permitted. If he was giving him a kitaba from desire to find money to pay off his own kitaba, that was permitted for him. Malik said that if a man had intercourse with a mukataba of his and she became pregnant by him, she had an option. If she liked she could be an umm walad. If she wished, she could confirm her kitaba. If she did not conceive, she still had her kitaba. Malik said, &quot;The generally agreed on way of doing things among us about a slave who is owned by two men is that one of them does not give a kitaba for his share, whether or not his companion gives him permission to do so, unless they both write the kitaba together, because that alone would effect setting him free. If the slave were to fulfil what he had agreed on to free half of himself, and then the one who had given a kitaba for half of him was not obliged to complete his setting free, that would be in opposition to the words of the Messenger of Allah, may Allah bless him and grant him peace. &#x27;If someone frees his share in a slave and has enough money to cover the full price of the slave, justly evaluated for him, he must give his partners their shares, so the slave is completely free . &#x27; &quot; Malik said, &quot;If he is not aware of that until the mukatab has met the terms or before he has met them the owner who has written him the kitaba returns what he has taken from the mukatab to him, and then he and his partner divide him according to their original shares and the kitaba is invalid. He is the slave of both of them in his original state.&quot; Malik spoke about a mukatab who was owned by two men and one of them granted him a delay in the payment of the right which he was owed, and the other refused to defer it, and so the one who refused to defer the payment exacted his part of the due. Malik said that if the mukatab then died and left property which did not complete his kitaba, &quot;They divide it according to what they are still owed by him. Each of them takes according to his share. If the mukatab leaves more than his kitaba, each of them takes what remains to them of the kitaba, and what remains after that is divided equally between them. If the mukatab is unable to pay his kitaba fully and the one who did not allow him to defer his payment has exacted more than his associate did, the slave is still divided equally between them, and he does not return to his associates the excess of what he has exacted, because he only exacted his right with the permission of his associate. If one of them remits what is owed to him and then his associate exacts part of what he is owed by him and then the mukatab is unable to pay, he belongs to both of them. And the one who has exacted something does not return anything because he only demanded what he was owed. That is like the debt of two men in one writing against one man. One of them grants him time to pay and the other is greedy and exacts his due. Then the debtor goes bankrupt. The one who exacted his due does not have to return any of what he took</p>

<p dir="ltr">[Muwatta Malik · 1491]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1491" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Muwatta Malik</a> · Translation origin: Human</p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="hi">
<h3>Hindi</h3>

<p dir="auto">इमाम मालिक ने हुमैद इब्न क़ैस अल-मक्की से रिवायत की के अल-मुतवक्किल के एक बेटे का एक मुकातब था जिस का इंतक़ाल मक्का में हुआ और वह अपनी बाक़ी मान्दा किताबत की रक़म छोड़ गया, उस पर लोगों का कुछ क़र्ज़ भी था और उसकी एक बेटी थी। हाकिम-ए-मक्का को फ़ैसला करने में कुछ तज़ब्ज़ुब था, चुनांचे उस ने अब्द अल-मलिक इब्न मरवान को ख़त लिखा। अब्द अल-मलिक ने जवाब दिया के सब से पहले लोगों के क़र्ज़ अदा करो, फिर किताबत की बाक़ी मान्दा रक़म अदा करो, फिर जो बचे उसे बेटी और आक़ा में तक्सीम कर दो। मालिक ने कहा के हमारे यहां यह मामूल है के आक़ा पर किताबत लिखना वाजिब नहीं अगर ग़ुलाम मुतालबा करे।</p>

<p dir="auto">[Muwatta Malik · 1491]</p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="ur">
<h3>Urdu</h3>

<p dir="rtl">امام مالک نے حمید بن قیس المکی سے روایت کی کہ المتاوکل کے ایک بیٹے کا ایک مکاتب تھا جس کا انتقال مکہ میں ہوا اور وہ اپنی باقی ماندہ کتابت کی رقم چھوڑ گیا، اس پر لوگوں کا کچھ قرض بھی تھا اور اس کی ایک بیٹی تھی۔ حاکم مکہ کو فیصلہ کرنے میں کچھ تذبذب تھا، چنانچہ اس نے عبدالملک بن مروان کو خط لکھا۔ عبدالملک نے جواب دیا کہ سب سے پہلے لوگوں کے قرض ادا کرو، پھر کتابت کی باقی ماندہ رقم ادا کرو، پھر جو بچے اسے بیٹی اور آقا میں تقسیم کر دو۔ مالک نے کہا کہ ہمارے یہاں یہ معمول ہے کہ آقا پر کتابت لکھنا واجب نہیں اگر غلام مطالبہ کرے۔</p>

<p dir="rtl">[Muwatta Malik · 1491]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1491" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Muwatta Malik</a> · Translation origin: Human</p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="ur-Latn">
<h3>Roman Urdu</h3>

<p dir="ltr">Imam Malik ne Humayd ibn Qays al-Makki se riwayat ki ke al-Mutawakkil ke ek bete ka ek mukatab tha jis ka inteqal Makka mein hua aur woh apni baqi manda kitabat ki raqam chhor gaya, us par logon ka kuch qarz bhi tha aur uski ek beti thi. Hakim-e-Makka ko faisla karne mein kuch tazabzub tha, chunanche us ne Abd al-Malik ibn Marwan ko khat likha. Abd al-Malik ne jawab diya ke sab se pehle logon ke qarz ada karo, phir kitabat ki baqi manda raqam ada karo, phir jo bache usay beti aur aaqa mein taqseem kar do. Malik ne kaha ke hamare yahan yeh mamool hai ke aaqa par kitabat likhna wajib nahi agar ghulam mutalba kare.</p>

<p dir="ltr">[Muwatta Malik · 1491]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1491" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Muwatta Malik</a> · Translation origin: Human</p>

## Narration 489

[Open this narration online](https://blaghtayt-alt.github.io/Hadith-Bank/?occurrence=deenhindi-malik%3A1493)

<p dir="auto"><strong>Chapter 39</strong></p>

<p><strong>Hadith</strong> · A narration preserved from the source.</p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="ar">
<h3>Arabic</h3>

<p dir="rtl">حَدَّثَنِي مَالِكٌ، أَنَّهُ بَلَغَهُ أَنَّ أُمَّ سَلَمَةَ، زَوْجَ النَّبِيِّ صلى الله عليه وسلم كَانَتْ تُقَاطِعُ مُكَاتَبِيهَا بِالذَّهَبِ وَالْوَرِقِ ‏.‏ قَالَ مَالِكٌ الأَمْرُ الْمُجْتَمَعُ عَلَيْهِ عِنْدَنَا فِي الْمَكَاتَبِ يَكُونُ بَيْنَ الشَّرِيكَيْنِ فَإِنَّهُ لاَ يَجُوزُ لأَحَدِهِمَا أَنْ يُقَاطِعَهُ عَلَى حِصَّتِهِ إِلاَّ بِإِذْنِ شَرِيكِهِ وَذَلِكَ أَنَّ الْعَبْدَ وَمَالَهُ بَيْنَهُمَا فَلاَ يَجُوزُ لأَحَدِهِمَا أَنْ يَأْخُذَ شَيْئًا مِنْ مَالِهِ إِلاَّ بِإِذْنِ شَرِيكِهِ وَلَوْ قَاطَعَهُ أَحَدُهُمَا دُونَ صَاحِبِهِ ثُمَّ حَازَ ذَلِكَ ثُمَّ مَاتَ الْمُكَاتَبُ وَلَهُ مَالٌ أَوْ عَجَزَ لَمْ يَكُنْ لِمَنْ قَاطَعَهُ شَىْءٌ مِنْ مَالِهِ وَلَمْ يَكُنْ لَهُ أَنْ يَرُدَّ مَا قَاطَعَهُ عَلَيْهِ وَيَرْجِعَ حَقُّهُ فِي رَقَبَتِهِ وَلَكِنْ مَنْ قَاطَعَ مُكَاتَبًا بِإِذْنِ شَرِيكِهِ ثُمَّ عَجَزَ الْمُكَاتَبُ فَإِنْ أَحَبَّ الَّذِي قَاطَعَهُ أَنْ يَرُدَّ الَّذِي أَخَذَ مِنْهُ مِنَ الْقَطَاعَةِ وَيَكُونُ عَلَى نَصِيبِهِ مِنْ رَقَبَةِ الْمُكَاتَبِ كَانَ ذَلِكَ لَهُ وَإِنْ مَاتَ الْمُكَاتَبُ وَتَرَكَ مَالاً اسْتَوْفَى الَّذِي بَقِيَتْ لَهُ الْكِتَابَةُ حَقَّهُ الَّذِي بَقِيَ لَهُ عَلَى الْمُكَاتَبِ مِنْ مَالِهِ ثُمَّ كَانَ مَا بَقِيَ مِنْ مَالِ الْمُكَاتَبِ بَيْنَ الَّذِي قَاطَعَهُ وَبَيْنَ شَرِيكِهِ عَلَى قَدْرِ حِصَصِهِمَا فِي الْمُكَاتَبِ وَإِنْ كَانَ أَحَدُهُمَا قَاطَعَهُ وَتَمَاسَكَ صَاحِبُهُ بِالْكِتَابَةِ ثُمَّ عَجَزَ الْمُكَاتَبُ قِيلَ لِلَّذِي قَاطَعَهُ إِنْ شِئْتَ أَنْ تَرُدَّ عَلَى صَاحِبِكَ نِصْفَ الَّذِي أَخَذْتَ وَيَكُونُ الْعَبْدُ بَيْنَكُمَا شَطْرَيْنِ وَإِنْ أَبَيْتَ فَجَمِيعُ الْعَبْدِ لِلَّذِي تَمَسَّكَ بِالرِّقِّ خَالِصًا ‏.‏ قَالَ مَالِكٌ فِي الْمُكَاتَبِ يَكُونُ بَيْنَ الرَّجُلَيْنِ فَيُقَاطِعُهُ أَحَدُهُمَا بِإِذْنِ صَاحِبِهِ ثُمَّ يَقْتَضِي الَّذِي تَمَسَّكَ بِالرِّقِّ مِثْلَ مَا قَاطَعَ عَلَيْهِ صَاحِبُهُ أَوْ أَكْثَرَ مِنْ ذَلِكَ ثُمَّ يَعْجِزُ الْمُكَاتَبُ ‏.‏ قَالَ مَالِكٌ فَهُوَ بَيْنَهُمَا لأَنَّهُ إِنَّمَا اقْتَضَى الَّذِي لَهُ عَلَيْهِ وَإِنِ اقْتَضَى أَقَلَّ مِمَّا أَخَذَ الَّذِي قَاطَعَهُ ثُمَّ عَجَزَ الْمُكَاتَبُ فَأَحَبَّ الَّذِي قَاطَعَهُ أَنَّ يَرُدَّ عَلَى صَاحِبِهِ نِصْفَ مَا تَفَضَّلَهُ بِهِ وَيَكُونُ الْعَبْدُ بَيْنَهُمَا نِصْفَيْنِ فَذَلِكَ لَهُ وَإِنْ أَبَى فَجَمِيعُ الْعَبْدِ لِلَّذِي لَمْ يُقَاطِعْهُ وَإِنْ مَاتَ الْمُكَاتَبُ وَتَرَكَ مَالاً فَأَحَبَّ الَّذِي قَاطَعَهُ أَنْ يَرُدَّ عَلَى صَاحِبِهِ نِصْفَ مَا تَفَضَّلَهُ بِهِ وَيَكُونُ الْمِيرَاثُ بَيْنَهُمَا فَذَلِكَ لَهُ وَإِنْ كَانَ الَّذِي تَمَسَّكَ بِالْكِتَابَةِ قَدْ أَخَذَ مِثْلَ مَا قَاطَعَ عَلَيْهِ شَرِيكُهُ أَوْ أَفْضَلَ فَالْمِيرَاثُ بَيْنَهُمَا بِقَدْرِ مِلْكِهِمَا لأَنَّهُ إِنَّمَا أَخَذَ حَقَّهُ ‏.‏ قَالَ مَالِكٌ فِي الْمُكَاتَبِ يَكُونُ بَيْنَ الرَّجُلَيْنِ فَيُقَاطِعُ أَحَدُهُمَا عَلَى نِصْفِ حَقِّهُ بِإِذْنِ صَاحِبِهِ ثُمَّ يَقْبِضُ الَّذِي تَمَسَّكَ بِالرِّقِّ أَقَلَّ مِمَّا قَاطَعَ عَلَيْهِ صَاحِبُهُ ثُمَّ يَعْجِزُ الْمُكَاتَبُ ‏.‏ قَالَ مَالِكٌ إِنْ أَحَبَّ الَّذِي قَاطَعَ الْعَبْدَ أَنْ يَرُدَّ عَلَى صَاحِبِهِ نِصْفَ مَا تَفَضَّلَهُ بِهِ كَانَ الْعَبْدُ بَيْنَهُمَا شَطْرَيْنِ وَإِنْ أَبَى أَنْ يَرُدَّ فَلِلَّذِي تَمَسَّكَ بِالرِّقِّ حِصَّةُ صَاحِبِهِ الَّذِي كَانَ قَاطَعَ عَلَيْهِ الْمُكَاتَبَ ‏.‏ قَالَ مَالِكٌ وَتَفْسِيرُ ذَلِكَ أَنَّ الْعَبْدَ يَكُونُ بَيْنَهُمَا شَطْرَيْنِ فَيُكَاتِبَانِهِ جَمِيعًا ثُمَّ يُقَاطِعُ أَحَدُهُمَا الْمُكَاتَبَ عَلَى نِصْفِ حَقِّهِ بِإِذْنِ صَاحِبِهِ وَذَلِكَ الرُّبُعُ مِنْ جَمِيعِ الْعَبْدِ ثُمَّ يَعْجِزُ الْمُكَاتَبُ فَيُقَالُ لِلَّذِي قَاطَعَهُ إِنْ شِئْتَ فَارْدُدْ عَلَى صَاحِبِكَ نِصْفَ مَا فَضَلْتَهُ بِهِ وَيَكُونُ الْعَبْدُ بَيْنَكُمَا شَطْرَيْنِ ‏.‏ وَإِنْ أَبَى كَانَ لِلَّذِي تَمَسَّكَ بِالْكِتَابَةِ رُبُعُ صَاحِبِهِ الَّذِي قَاطَعَ الْمُكَاتَبَ عَلَيْهِ خَالِصًا وَكَانَ لَهُ نِصْفُ الْعَبْدِ فَذَلِكَ ثَلاَثَةُ أَرْبَاعِ الْعَبْدِ وَكَانَ لِلَّذِي قَاطَعَ رُبُعُ الْعَبْدِ لأَنَّهُ أَبَى أَنْ يَرُدَّ ثَمَنَ رُبُعِهِ الَّذِي قَاطَعَ عَلَيْهِ ‏.‏ قَالَ مَالِكٌ فِي الْمُكَاتَبِ يُقَاطِعُهُ سَيِّدُهُ فَيَعْتِقُ وَيَكْتُبُ عَلَيْهِ مَا بَقِيَ مِنْ قَطَاعَتِهِ دَيْنًا عَلَيْهِ ثُمَّ يَمُوتُ الْمُكَاتَبُ وَعَلَيْهِ دَيْنٌ لِلنَّاسِ ‏.‏ قَالَ مَالِكٌ فَإِنَّ سَيِّدَهُ لاَ يُحَاصُّ غُرَمَاءَهُ بِالَّذِي عَلَيْهِ مِنْ قَطَاعَتِهِ وَلِغُرَمَائِهِ أَنْ يُبَدَّءُوا عَلَيْهِ ‏.‏ قَالَ مَالِكٌ لَيْسَ لِلْمُكَاتَبِ أَنْ يُقَاطِعَ سَيِّدَهُ إِذَا كَانَ عَلَيْهِ دَيْنٌ لِلنَّاسِ فَيَعْتِقُ وَيَصِيرُ لاَ شَىْءَ لَهُ لأَنَّ أَهْلَ الدَّيْنِ أَحَقُّ بِمَالِهِ مِنْ سَيِّدِهِ فَلَيْسَ ذَلِكَ بِجَائِزٍ لَهُ ‏.‏ قَالَ مَالِكٌ الأَمْرُ عِنْدَنَا فِي الرَّجُلِ يُكَاتِبُ عَبْدَهُ ثُمَّ يُقَاطِعُهُ بِالذَّهَبِ فَيَضَعُ عَنْهُ مِمَّا عَلَيْهِ مِنَ الْكِتَابَةِ عَلَى أَنْ يُعَجِّلَ لَهُ مَا قَاطَعَهُ عَلَيْهِ أَنَّهُ لَيْسَ بِذَلِكَ بَأْسٌ وَإِنَّمَا كَرِهَ ذَلِكَ مَنْ كَرِهَهُ لأَنَّهُ أَنْزَلَهُ بِمَنْزِلَةِ الدَّيْنِ يَكُونُ لِلرَّجُلِ عَلَى الرَّجُلِ إِلَى أَجَلٍ فَيَضَعُ عَنْهُ وَيَنْقُدُهُ وَلَيْسَ هَذَا مِثْلَ الدَّيْنِ إِنَّمَا كَانَتْ قَطَاعَةُ الْمُكَاتَبِ سَيِّدَهُ عَلَى أَنْ يُعْطِيَهُ مَالاً فِي أَنْ يَتَعَجَّلَ الْعِتْقَ فَيَجِبُ لَهُ الْمِيرَاثُ وَالشَّهَادَةُ وَالْحُدُودُ وَتَثْبُتُ لَهُ حُرْمَةُ الْعَتَاقَةِ وَلَمْ يَشْتَرِ دَرَاهِمَ بِدَرَاهِمَ وَلاَ ذَهَبًا بِذَهَبٍ وَإِنَّمَا مَثَلُ ذَلِكَ مَثَلُ رَجُلٍ قَالَ لِغُلاَمِهِ ائْتِنِي بِكَذَا وَكَذَا دِينَارًا وَأَنْتَ حُرٌّ فَوَضَعَ عَنْهُ مِنْ ذَلِكَ فَقَالَ إِنْ جِئْتَنِي بِأَقَلَّ مِنْ ذَلِكَ فَأَنْتَ حُرٌّ ‏.‏ فَلَيْسَ هَذَا دَيْنًا ثَابِتًا وَلَوْ كَانَ دَيْنًا ثَابِتًا لَحَاصَّ بِهِ السَّيِّدُ غُرَمَاءَ الْمُكَاتَبِ إِذَا مَاتَ أَوْ أَفْلَسَ فَدَخَلَ مَعَهُمْ فِي مَالِ مُكَاتَبِهِ ‏.‏</p>

<p dir="rtl">[Muwatta Malik · 1493]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1493" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Muwatta Malik</a></p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="en">
<h3>English</h3>

<p dir="ltr">Malik said, &quot;The generally agreed on way of doing things among us is that when slaves write their kitaba together in one kitaba, and some are responsible for others, and they are not reduced anything by the death of one of the responsible ones, and then one of them says, &#x27;I can&#x27;t do it,&#x27; and gives up, his companions can use him in whatever work he can do and they help each other with that in their kitaba until they are freed, if they are freed, or remain slaves if they remain slaves.&quot; Malik said, &quot;The generally agreed on way of doing things among us is that when a master gives a slave his kitaba, it is not permitted for the master to let anyone assume the responsibility for the kitaba of his slave if the slave dies or is incapable. This is not part of the sunna of the muslims. That is because when a man assumes responsibility to the master of a mukatab for what the mukatab owes of his kitaba, and then the master of the mukatab pursues that from the one who assumes the responsibility, he takes his money falsely. It is not as if he is buying the mukatab, so that what he gives is part of the price of something that is his, and neither is the mukatab being freed so that the price established for him buys his inviolability as a free man. If the mukatab is unable to meet the payments he reverts to his master and is his slave. That is because kitaba is not a fixed debt which can be assumed by the master of the mukatab. It is something which, when it is paid by the mukatab, sets him free. If the mukatab dies and has a debt, his master is not one of the creditors for what remains unpaid of the kitaba. The creditors have precedence over the master. If the mukatab cannot meet the payments, and he owes debts to people, he reverts to being a slave owned by his master and the debts to the people are the liability of the mukatab. The creditors do not enter with the master into any share of the price of his person.&quot; Malik said, &quot;When people are written together in one kitaba and there is no kinship between them by which they inherit from each other, and some of them are responsible for others, then none of them are freed before the others until all the kitaba has been paid. If one of them dies and leaves property and it is more than all of what is against them, it pays all that is against them . The excess of the property goes to the master, and none of those who have been written in the kitaba with the deceased have any of the excess. The master&#x27;s claims are overshadowed by their claims for the portions which remain against them of the kitaba which can be fulfilled from the property of the deceased, because the deceased had assumed their responsibility and they must use his property to pay for their freedom. If the deceased mukatab has a free child not born in kitaba and who was not written in the kitaba, it does not inherit from him because the mukatab was not freed until he died.&quot; Malik related to me that he heard that Umm Salama, the wife of the Prophet, may Allah bless him and grant him peace, made a settlement with her mukatab for an agreed amount of gold and silver. Malik said, &quot;The generally agreed on way of doing things among us in the case of a mukatab who is shared by two partners, is that one of them cannot make a settlement with him for an agreed price according to his portion without the consent of his partner. That is because the slave and his property are owned by both of them, and so one of them is not permitted to take any of the property except with the consent of his partner. If one of them settled with the mukatab and his partner did not, and he took the agreed price, and then the mukatab died while he had property or was unable to pay, the one who settled would not have anything of the mukatab&#x27;s property and he could not return that for which he made settlement so that his right to the slave&#x27;s person would return to him. However, when someone settles with a mukatab with the permission of his partner and then the mukatab is unable to pay, it is preferable that the one who broke with him return what he has taken from the mukatab for the severance and he can have back his portion of the mukatab. He can do that. If the mukatab dies and leaves property, the partner who has kept hold of the kitaba is paid in full the amount of the kitaba which remains to him against the mukatab from the mukatab&#x27;s property. Then what remains of property of the mukatab is between the partner who broke with him and his partner, according to their shares in the mukatab. If one of the partners breaks off with him and the other keeps the kitaba, and the mukatab is unable to pay, it is said to the partner who settled with him, &#x27;If you wish to give your partner half of what you took so the slave is divided between you, then do so. If you refuse, then all of the slave belongs to the one who held on to possession of the slave.&#x27; &quot; Malik spoke about a mukatab who was shared between two men and one of them made a settlement with him with the permission of his partner. Then the one who retained possession of the slave demanded the like of that for which his partner had settled or more than that and the mukatab could not pay it. He said, &quot;The mukatab is shared between them because the man has only demanded what is owed to him. If he demands less than what the one who settled with him took and the mukatab can not manage that, and the one who settled with him prefers to return to his partner half of what he took so the slave is divided in halves between them, he can do that. If he refuses then all of the slave belongs to the one who did not settle with him. If the mukatab dies and leaves property, and the one who settled with him prefers to return to his companion half of what he has taken so the inheritance is divided between them, he can do that. If the one who has kept the kitaba takes the like of what the one who has settled with him took, or more, the inheritance is between them according to their shares in the slave because he is only taking his right.&quot; Malik spoke about a mukatab who was shared between two men and one of them made a settlement with him for half of what was due to him with the permission of his partner, and then the one who retained possession of the slave took less than what his partner settled with him for and the mukatab was unable to pay. He said, &quot;If the one who made a settlement with the slave prefers to return half of what he was awarded to his partner, the slave is divided between them. If he refuses to return it, the one who retained possession has the portion of the share for which his partner made a settlement with the mukatab.&quot; Malik said, &quot;The explanation of that is that the slave is divided in two halves between them. They write him a kitaba together and then one of them makes a settlement with the mukatab for half his due with the permission of his partner. That is a fourth of all the slave. Then the mukatab is unable to continue, so it is said to the one who settled with him, &#x27;If you wish, return to your partner half of what you were awarded and the slave is divided equally between you.&#x27; If he refuses, the one who held to the kitaba takes in full the fourth of his partner for which he made settlement with the mukatab. He had half the slave, so that now gives him three-fourths of the slave. The one who broke off has a fourth of the slave because he refused to return the equivalent of the fourth share for which he settled.&quot; Malik spoke about a mukatab whose master made a settlement with him and set him free and what remained of his severance was written against him as debt, then the mukatab died and people had debts against him. He said, &quot;His master does not share with the creditors because of what he is owed from the severance. The creditors begin first.&quot; Malik said, &quot;A mukatab cannot break with his master when he owes debts to people. He would be set free and have nothing because the people who hold the debts are more entitled to his property than his master. That is not permitted for him.&quot; Malik said, &quot;According to the way things are done among us, there is no harm if a man gives a kitaba to his slave and settles with him for gold and reduces what he is owed of the kitaba provided that only the gold is paid immediately. Whoever disapproves of that does so because he puts it in the category of a debt which a man has against another man for a set term. He gives him a reduction and he pays it immediately. This is not like that debt. The breaking of the mukatab with his master is dependent on his giving money to speed up the setting free. Inheritance, testimony and the hudud are obliged for him and the inviolability of being set free is established for him. He is not buying dirhams for dirhams or gold for gold. Rather it is like a man who having said to his slave, &#x27;Bring me such-and-such an amount of dinars and you are free&#x27;, then reduces that for him, saying, &#x27;If you bring me less than that, you are free.&#x27; That is not a fixed debt. Had it been a fixed debt, the master would have shared with the creditors of the mukatab when he died or went bankrupt. His claim on the property of the mukatab would join theirs</p>

<p dir="ltr">[Muwatta Malik · 1493]</p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="hi">
<h3>Hindi</h3>

<p dir="auto">इमाम मालिक ने फ़रमाया के हमारे यहां मुत्तफ़िक़ा तरीक़ा यह है के जब ग़ुलाम इकट्ठे किताबत लिखवाएं, और एक की ज़िम्मेदारी दूसरे पर हो, तो एक के मरने से उनकी ज़िम्मेदारी कम नहीं होती। अगर एक कहे के &#x27;मैं नहीं कर सकता&#x27; और हिम्मत हार दे, तो उसके साथी उस से वह काम ले सकते हैं जो वह कर सके, और वह किताबत में एक दूसरे की मदद करेंगे।</p>

<p dir="auto">[Muwatta Malik · 1493]</p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="ur">
<h3>Urdu</h3>

<p dir="rtl">امام مالک نے فرمایا کہ ہمارے یہاں متفقہ طریقہ یہ ہے کہ جب غلام اکٹھے کتابت لکھوائیں، اور ایک کی ذمہ داری دوسرے پر ہو، تو ایک کے مرنے سے ان کی ذمہ داری کم نہیں ہوتی۔ اگر ایک کہے کہ &#x27;میں نہیں کر سکتا&#x27; اور ہمت ہار دے، تو اس کے ساتھی اس سے وہ کام لے سکتے ہیں جو وہ کر سکے، اور وہ کتابت میں ایک دوسرے کی مدد کریں گے۔</p>

<p dir="rtl">[Muwatta Malik · 1493]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1493" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Muwatta Malik</a> · Translation origin: Human</p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="ur-Latn">
<h3>Roman Urdu</h3>

<p dir="ltr">Imam Malik ne farmaya ke hamare yahan muttafiqa tareeqa yeh hai ke jab ghulam ikathay kitabat likhwayein, aur ek ki zimmedari dusre par ho, to ek ke marne se unki zimmedari kam nahi hoti. Agar ek kahe ke &#x27;main nahi kar sakta&#x27; aur himmat haar de, to uske saathi us se woh kaam le sakte hain jo woh kar sake, aur woh kitabat mein ek dusre ki madad karenge.</p>

<p dir="ltr">[Muwatta Malik · 1493]</p>

## Narration 490

[Open this narration online](https://blaghtayt-alt.github.io/Hadith-Bank/?occurrence=deenhindi-malik%3A1500)

<p dir="auto"><strong>Chapter 41</strong></p>

<p><strong>Hadith</strong> · A narration preserved from the source.</p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="ar">
<h3>Arabic</h3>

<p dir="rtl">حَدَّثَنَا مَالِكٌ، عَنْ نَافِعٍ، عَنْ عَبْدِ اللَّهِ بْنِ عُمَرَ، أَنَّهُ قَالَ جَاءَتِ الْيَهُودُ إِلَى رَسُولِ اللَّهِ صلى الله عليه وسلم فَذَكَرُوا لَهُ أَنَّ رَجُلاً مِنْهُمْ وَامْرَأَةً زَنَيَا فَقَالَ لَهُمْ رَسُولُ اللَّهِ صلى الله عليه وسلم ‏ &quot;‏ مَا تَجِدُونَ فِي التَّوْرَاةِ فِي شَأْنِ الرَّجْمِ ‏&quot;‏ ‏.‏ فَقَالُوا نَفْضَحُهُمْ وَيُجْلَدُونَ ‏.‏ فَقَالَ عَبْدُ اللَّهِ بْنُ سَلاَمٍ كَذَبْتُمْ إِنَّ فِيهَا الرَّجْمَ ‏.‏ فَأَتَوْا بِالتَّوْرَاةِ فَنَشَرُوهَا فَوَضَعَ أَحَدُهُمْ يَدَهُ عَلَى آيَةِ الرَّجْمِ ثُمَّ قَرَأَ مَا قَبْلَهَا وَمَا بَعْدَهَا فَقَالَ لَهُ عَبْدُ اللَّهِ بْنُ سَلاَمٍ ارْفَعْ يَدَكَ فَرَفَعَ يَدَهُ فَإِذَا فِيهَا آيَةُ الرَّجْمِ فَقَالُوا صَدَقَ يَا مُحَمَّدُ فِيهَا آيَةُ الرَّجْمِ ‏.‏ فَأَمَرَ بِهِمَا رَسُولُ اللَّهِ صلى الله عليه وسلم فَرُجِمَا ‏.‏ فَقَالَ عَبْدُ اللَّهِ بْنُ عُمَرَ فَرَأَيْتُ الرَّجُلَ يَحْنِي عَلَى الْمَرْأَةِ يَقِيهَا الْحِجَارَةَ ‏.‏</p>

<p dir="rtl">[Muwatta Malik · 1500]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1500" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Muwatta Malik</a></p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="en">
<h3>English</h3>

<p dir="ltr">Malik said in the case of an umm walad who injured someone, &quot;The blood-money of that injury is the responsibility of her master from his property, unless the blood-money of the injury is greater than the value of the umm walad. Her master does not have to pay more than her value. That is because when the master of a slave or slave-girl surrenders his slave or slave-girl for an injury which one of them has done, he does not owe any more than that, even if the blood-money is greater. As the master of the umm walad cannot surrender her because of the precedent of the sunna, when he pays her price, it is as if he had surrendered her. He does not have to pay more than that. This is the best of what I have heard about the matter. The master is not obliged to assume responsibility for more than an umm walad&#x27;s value because of her criminal action.&quot; Malik related to me from Nafi that Abdullah ibn Umar said, &quot;The Jews came to the Messenger of Allah, may Allah bless him and grant him peace, and mentioned to him that a man and woman from among them had committed adultery. The Messenger of Allah, may Allah bless him and grant him peace, asked them, &#x27;What do you find in the Torah about stoning?&#x27; They said, &#x27;We make their wrong action known and flog them.&#x27; Abdullah ibn Salam said, &#x27;You have lied! It has stoning for it, so bring the Torah.&#x27; They spread it out and one of them placed his hand over the ayat of stoning. Then he read what was before it and what was after it. Abdullah ibn Salam told him to lift his hand. He lifted his hand and there was the ayat of stoning. They said, &#x27;He has spoken the truth, Muhammad. The ayat of stoning is in it.&#x27; So the Messenger of Allah, may Allah bless him and grant him peace, gave the order and they were stoned . &quot; Abdullah ibn Umar added, &quot;I saw the man leaning over the woman to protect her from the stones.&quot; Malik commented, &quot;By leaning he meant throwing himself over her so that the stones fell on him</p>

<p dir="ltr">[Muwatta Malik · 1500]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1500" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Muwatta Malik</a> · Translation origin: Human</p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="hi">
<h3>Hindi</h3>

<p dir="auto">अब्दुल्लाह बिन उम्र (रज़ियल्लाहु अन्हु) से रिवायत है के यहूदि रसूल अल्लाह (सल्लल्लाहु अलैहि वसल्लम) के पास आये और बयां किया कि हम में से एक मर्द और औरत ने ज़िना किया तोह रसूल अल्लाह (सल्लल्लाहु अलैहि वसल्लम) ने फ़रमाया के तौरात में क्या हुक्म है राजम का? यहूदियों ने कहा हम में जो कोई ज़िना करे उसको हम रुस्वा करते हैं और कोड़े मारते हैं. अब्दुल्लाह बिन सलाम ने कहा तुम झूठ बोलते हो को खोला और एक शख्स ने उन में से अपना हाथ राजम की आयत पर रख लिया और उसके अव्वल और आखिर की आयतें पढ़ीं. अब्दुल्लाह बिन सलाम ने उस से कहा अपना हाथ उठा कहने लगे के सच कहा अब्दुल्लाह बिन सलाम ने वसल्लम) ने हुक्म किया राजम का तोह वह मर्द और औरत राजम किये गए. अब्दुल्लाह बिन उम्र (रज़ियल्लाहु अन्हु) ने कहा के मैं ने मर्द को देखा के वह औरत की तरफ झुकता था उसको बचाने पत्रों से.</p>

<p dir="auto">[Muwatta Malik · 1500]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1500" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Muwatta Malik</a> · Translation origin: Human</p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="ur">
<h3>Urdu</h3>

<p dir="rtl">عبداللہ بن عمر رضی اللہ تعالیٰ عنہ سے روایت ہے کہ یہودی رسول اللہ کے پاس آئے اور بیان کیا کہ ہم میں سے ایک مرد اور عورت نے زنا کیا تو رسول اللہ صلی اللہ علیہ وآلہ وسلم نے فرمایا کہ تورات میں کیا حکم ہے رجم کا؟ یہودیوں نے کہا ہم میں جو کوئی زنا کرے اس کو ہم رسوا کرتے ہیں اور کوڑے مارتے ہیں عبداللہ بن سلام نے کہا تم جھوٹ بولتے ہو تورات میں رجم ہے لاؤ تم تورات کو پڑھو اس کو ، انہوں نے تورات کو کھولا اور ایک شخص نے ان میں سے اپنا ہاتھ رجم کی آیت پر رکھ لیا اور اس کے اول اور آخر کی آیتیں پڑھیں عبداللہ بن سلام نے اس سے کہا اپنا ہاتھ اٹھا اس نے جو ہاتھ اٹھایا تو رجم کی آیت نکلی تب سب یہودی کہنے لگے کہ سچ کہا عبداللہ بن سلام نے آیت رجم کی موجود ہے پھر رسول اللہ صلی اللہ علیہ وآلہ وسلم نے حکم کیا رجم کا تو وہ مرد اور عورت رجم کئے گئے عبداللہ بن عمر نے کہا کہ میں نے مرد کو دیکھا کہ وہ عورت کی طرف جھکتا تھا اس کو بچانے پتھروں سے۔</p>

<p dir="rtl">[Muwatta Malik · 1500]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1500" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Muwatta Malik</a> · Translation origin: Human</p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="ur-Latn">
<h3>Roman Urdu</h3>

<p dir="ltr">Abdullah bin Umar (Radi Allahu Anhu) se riwayat hai ke yahudi Rasool Allah (Sallallahu Alaihi Wasallam) ke paas aaye aur bayan kiya ke hum mein se ek mard aur aurat ne zina kiya toh Rasool Allah (Sallallahu Alaihi Wasallam) ne farmaya ke Taurat mein kya hukm hai rajam ka? Yahudiyon ne kaha hum mein jo koi zina kare usko hum ruswa karte hain aur kode maarte hain. Abdullah bin Salam ne kaha tum jhoot bolte ho, Taurat mein rajam hai, lao tum Taurat ko padho isko. Unhone Taurat ko khola aur ek shakhs ne un mein se apna haath rajam ki aayat par rakh liya aur uske awwal aur aakhir ki aayatein padhin. Abdullah bin Salam ne us se kaha apna haath utha, usne jo haath uthaya toh rajam ki aayat nikli. Tab sab yahudi kehne lage ke sach kaha Abdullah bin Salam ne, aayat-e-rajam ki maujood hai. Phir Rasool Allah (Sallallahu Alaihi Wasallam) ne hukm kiya rajam ka toh woh mard aur aurat rajam kiye gaye. Abdullah bin Umar (Radi Allahu Anhu) ne kaha ke main ne mard ko dekha ke woh aurat ki taraf jhukta tha usko bachane pathron se.</p>

<p dir="ltr">[Muwatta Malik · 1500]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1500" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Muwatta Malik</a> · Translation origin: Human</p>

## Narration 491

[Open this narration online](https://blaghtayt-alt.github.io/Hadith-Bank/?occurrence=deenhindi-malik%3A1501)

<p dir="auto"><strong>Chapter 41</strong></p>

<p><strong>Hadith</strong> · A narration preserved from the source.</p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="ar">
<h3>Arabic</h3>

<p dir="rtl">حَدَّثَنِي مَالِكٌ، عَنْ يَحْيَى بْنِ سَعِيدٍ، عَنْ سَعِيدِ بْنِ الْمُسَيَّبِ، أَنَّ رَجُلاً، مِنْ أَسْلَمَ جَاءَ إِلَى أَبِي بَكْرٍ الصِّدِّيقِ فَقَالَ لَهُ إِنَّ الأَخِرَ زَنَا ‏.‏ فَقَالَ لَهُ أَبُو بَكْرٍ هَلْ ذَكَرْتَ هَذَا لأَحَدٍ غَيْرِي فَقَالَ لاَ ‏.‏ فَقَالَ أَبُو بَكْرٍ فَتُبْ إِلَى اللَّهِ وَاسْتَتِرْ بِسِتْرِ اللَّهِ فَإِنَّ اللَّهَ يَقْبَلُ التَّوْبَةَ عَنْ عِبَادِهِ ‏.‏ فَلَمْ تُقْرِرْهُ نَفْسُهُ حَتَّى أَتَى عُمَرَ بْنَ الْخَطَّابِ فَقَالَ لَهُ مِثْلَ مَا قَالَ لأَبِي بَكْرٍ فَقَالَ لَهُ عُمَرُ مِثْلَ مَا قَالَ لَهُ أَبُو بَكْرٍ فَلَمْ تُقْرِرْهُ نَفْسُهُ حَتَّى جَاءَ إِلَى رَسُولِ اللَّهِ صلى الله عليه وسلم فَقَالَ لَهُ إِنَّ الأَخِرَ زَنَا فَقَالَ سَعِيدٌ فَأَعْرَضَ عَنْهُ رَسُولُ اللَّهِ صلى الله عليه وسلم ثَلاَثَ مَرَّاتٍ كُلُّ ذَلِكَ يُعْرِضُ عَنْهُ رَسُولُ اللَّهِ صلى الله عليه وسلم حَتَّى إِذَا أَكْثَرَ عَلَيْهِ بَعَثَ رَسُولُ اللَّهِ صلى الله عليه وسلم إِلَى أَهْلِهِ فَقَالَ ‏&quot;‏ أَيَشْتَكِي أَمْ بِهِ جِنَّةٌ ‏&quot;‏ ‏.‏ فَقَالُوا يَا رَسُولَ اللَّهِ وَاللَّهِ إِنَّهُ لَصَحِيحٌ ‏.‏ فَقَالَ رَسُولُ اللَّهِ صلى الله عليه وسلم ‏&quot;‏ أَبِكْرٌ أَمْ ثَيِّبٌ ‏&quot;‏ ‏.‏ فَقَالُوا بَلْ ثَيِّبٌ يَا رَسُولَ اللَّهِ ‏.‏ فَأَمَرَ بِهِ رَسُولُ اللَّهِ صلى الله عليه وسلم فَرُجِمَ ‏.‏</p>

<p dir="rtl">[Muwatta Malik · 1501]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1501" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Muwatta Malik</a></p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="en">
<h3>English</h3>

<p dir="ltr">Malik related to me from Yahya ibn Said from Said ibn al-Musayyab that a man from the Aslam tribe came to Abu Bakr as-Siddiq and said to him, &quot;I have committed adultery.&quot; Abu Bakr said to him, &quot;Have you mentioned this to anyone else?&quot; He said, &quot;No.&quot; Abu Bakr said to him, &quot;Then cover it up with the veil of Allah. Allah accepts tawba from his slaves.&quot; His self was still unsettled, so he went to Umar ibn al- Khattab. He told him the same as he had said to Abu Bakr, and Umar told him the same as Abu Bakr had said to him. His self was still not settled so he went to the Messenger of Allah, may Allah bless him and grant him peace, and said to him, &quot;I have committed adultery,&quot; insistently. The Messenger of Allah, may Allah bless him and grant him peace, turned away from him three times. Each time the Messenger of Allah, may Allah bless him and grant him peace, turned away from him until it became too much. The Messenger of Allah, may Allah bless him and grant him peace, questioned his family, &quot;Does he have an illness which affects his mind, or is he mad?&quot; They said, &quot;Messenger of Allah, by Allah, he is well.&quot; The Messenger of Allah, may Allah bless him and grant him peace, said, &quot;Unmarried or married?&quot; They said, &quot;Married, Messenger of Allah.&quot; The Messenger of Allah, may Allah bless him and grant him peace, gave the order and he was stoned</p>

<p dir="ltr">[Muwatta Malik · 1501]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1501" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Muwatta Malik</a> · Translation origin: Human</p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="hi">
<h3>Hindi</h3>

<p dir="auto">सईद बिन मुसय्यब से रिवायत है के क़बीला असलम का एक शख्स अबू बक्र सिद्दीक़ (रज़ियल्लाहु अन्हु) के पास आया और कहा के इस नालायक़ ने (अपनी तरफ इशारा करके) जीना किया. अबू बक्र (रज़ियल्लाहु अन्हु) ने कहा: क्या तूने ये बात किसी और से बयां की है? बोलै: नहीं. अबू बक्र (रज़ियल्लाहु अन्हु) ने कहा: तू अल्लाह से तौबा कर और अल्लाह के परदे में छुपा रह क्युंके अल्लाह जल्ला जलालूहू अपने बन्दों की तौबा कबूल करता है. उससे तस्कीन न हुई अन्हु) के पास गया वही जवाब दिया. फिर भी तस्कीन न हुई तो वो रसूल अल्लाह (सल्लल्लाहु अलैहि वसल्लम) के पास आया और कहा: इस नालायक़ ने ज़िना किया है. तीन बार उसने कहा और तीनों बार रसूल अल्लाह (सल्लल्लाहु अलैहि वसल्लम) ने उसकी तरफ से मुंह फेर लिया. जब बहुत बार उसने कहा तो रसूल अल्लाह (सल्लल्लाहु अलैहि वसल्लम) ने लोगों से फ़रमाया: क्या ये बीमार हो गया है या इससे जूनून है? लोगों ने अर्ज़ किया: या रसूल अल्लाह (सल्लल्लाहु अलैहि वसल्लम) (सल्लल्लाहु अलैहि वसल्लम) ने पूछा: क्या इसका निकाह हुआ है? लोगों ने कहा: हाँ. तोह आप (सल्लल्लाहु अलैहि वसल्लम) ने इसको संगसार करने का हुकुम दिया और वो संगसार कर दिया गया.</p>

<p dir="auto">[Muwatta Malik · 1501]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1501" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Muwatta Malik</a> · Translation origin: Human</p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="ur">
<h3>Urdu</h3>

<p dir="rtl">سعید بن مسیب سے روایت ہے کہ ایک شخص اسلم کے قبیلے کا ابوبکر صدیق رضی اللہ تعالیٰ عنہ کے پاس آیا اور کہا کہ اس نالائق نے (اپنی طرف اشارہ کرکے) زنا کیا ابوبکر رضی اللہ تعالیٰ عنہ نے کہا تو نے یہ بات اور کسی سے تو بیان نہیں کیا بولا نہیں ابوبکر رضی اللہ تعالیٰ عنہ نے کہا تو توبہ کر اللہ سے اور چھپا رہ اللہ کے پردے میں کیونکہ اللہ جل جلالہ توبہ قبول کرتا ہے اپنے بندوں کی اس کو تسکین نہ ہوئی وہ حضرت عمر رضی اللہ تعالیٰ عنہ کے پاس آیا حضرت عمر سے بھی ایسا ہی کہا جیسا کہ ابوبکر رضی اللہ تعالیٰ عنہ سے کہا تھا حضرت عمر نے بھی وہی جواب دیا پھر بھی اس کو تسکین نہ ہوئی پھر وہ رسول اللہ صلی اللہ علیہ وآلہ وسلم کے پاس آیا اور کہا کہ اس نالائق نے زنا کیا تین بار اس نے کہا اور تینوں بار رسول اللہ صلی اللہ علیہ وآلہ وسلم نے اس کی طرف سے منہ پھیر لیا جب بہت اس نے کہا تو رسول اللہ صلی اللہ علیہ وآلہ وسلم نے لوگوں سے فرمایا کیا یہ بیمار ہوگیا ہے یا اس کو جنون ہے لوگوں نے عرض کیا یا رسول اللہ صلی اللہ علیہ وآلہ وسلم وہ تندرست ہے آپ صلی اللہ علیہ وآلہ وسلم نے فرمایا اس کا نکاح ہوا ہے یا نہیں لوگوں نے کہا ہوا ہے تو آپ صلی اللہ علیہ وآلہ وسلم نے حکم کیا اس کو سنگسار کرنے کا وہ سنگسار کر دیا گیا ۔</p>

<p dir="rtl">[Muwatta Malik · 1501]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1501" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Muwatta Malik</a> · Translation origin: Human</p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="ur-Latn">
<h3>Roman Urdu</h3>

<p dir="ltr">Saeed bin Musayyab se riwayat hai ke qabeela Aslam ka ek shakhs Abu Bakr Siddiq (Radi Allahu Anhu) ke paas aaya aur kaha ke is nalayq ne (apni taraf ishara karke) zina kiya. Abu Bakr (Radi Allahu Anhu) ne kaha: Kya tune ye baat kisi aur se bayan ki hai? Bola: Nahi. Abu Bakr (Radi Allahu Anhu) ne kaha: Tu Allah se tauba kar aur Allah ke parde mein chupa reh, kyunke Allah Jalla Jalaluhu apne bandon ki tauba qabool karta hai. Usse taskeen na hui, wo Hazrat Umar (Radi Allahu Anhu) ke paas gaya, unse bhi wahi kaha jo Abu Bakr (Radi Allahu Anhu) se kaha tha. Hazrat Umar (Radi Allahu Anhu) ne bhi wahi jawab diya. Phir bhi taskeen na hui to wo Rasool Allah (Sallallahu Alaihi Wasallam) ke paas aaya aur kaha: Is nalayq ne zina kiya hai. Teen baar usne kaha aur teeno baar Rasool Allah (Sallallahu Alaihi Wasallam) ne uski taraf se munh pher liya. Jab bahut baar usne kaha to Rasool Allah (Sallallahu Alaihi Wasallam) ne logon se farmaya: Kya ye bimar ho gaya hai ya isse junoon hai? Logon ne arz kiya: Ya Rasool Allah (Sallallahu Alaihi Wasallam), wo tandurust hai. Aap (Sallallahu Alaihi Wasallam) ne pucha: Kya iska nikah hua hai? Logon ne kaha: Haan. Toh Aap (Sallallahu Alaihi Wasallam) ne isko sangsaar karne ka hukum diya aur wo sangsaar kar diya gaya.</p>

<p dir="ltr">[Muwatta Malik · 1501]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1501" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Muwatta Malik</a> · Translation origin: Human</p>

## Narration 492

[Open this narration online](https://blaghtayt-alt.github.io/Hadith-Bank/?occurrence=deenhindi-malik%3A1502)

<p dir="auto"><strong>Chapter 41</strong></p>

<p><strong>Hadith</strong> · A narration preserved from the source.</p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="ar">
<h3>Arabic</h3>

<p dir="rtl">حَدَّثَنِي مَالِكٌ، عَنْ يَحْيَى بْنِ سَعِيدٍ، عَنْ سَعِيدِ بْنِ الْمُسَيَّبِ، أَنَّهُ قَالَ بَلَغَنِي أَنَّ رَسُولَ اللَّهِ صلى الله عليه وسلم قَالَ لِرَجُلٍ مِنْ أَسْلَمَ يُقَالُ لَهُ هَزَّالٌ ‏ &quot;‏ يَا هَزَّالُ لَوْ سَتَرْتَهُ بِرِدَائِكَ لَكَانَ خَيْرًا لَكَ ‏&quot;‏ ‏.‏ قَالَ يَحْيَى بْنُ سَعِيدٍ فَحَدَّثْتُ بِهَذَا الْحَدِيثِ فِي مَجْلِسٍ فِيهِ يَزِيدُ بْنُ نُعَيْمِ بْنِ هَزَّالٍ الأَسْلَمِيِّ فَقَالَ يَزِيدُ هَزَّالٌ جَدِّي وَهَذَا الْحَدِيثُ حَقٌّ ‏.‏</p>

<p dir="rtl">[Muwatta Malik · 1502]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1502" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Muwatta Malik</a></p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="en">
<h3>English</h3>

<p dir="ltr">Malik related to me from Yahya ibn Said that Said ibn al-Musayyab said, &quot;I have heard that the Messenger of Allah, may Allah bless him and grant him peace, said to a man from the Aslam tribe called Hazzal, &#x27;Hazzal, had you veiled him with your cloak, it would have been better for you.&#x27; &quot; Yahya ibn Said said, &quot;I related this hadith in an assembly among whom was Yazid ibn Nuaym ibn Hazzal al-Aslami. Yazid said, &#x27;Hazzal was my grandfather. This hadith is true</p>

<p dir="ltr">[Muwatta Malik · 1502]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1502" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Muwatta Malik</a> · Translation origin: Human</p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="hi">
<h3>Hindi</h3>

<p dir="auto">सईद बिन मुसय्यब से रिवायत है के रसूल अल्लाह (सल्लल्लाहु अलैहि वसल्लम) ने उस शख़्स (जो क़बीला असलम से था और उसका नाम हज़्ज़ाल था) से कहा: ऐ हज़्ज़ाल, अगर तू इस ख़बर को छुपा लेता तो तेरे लिए बेहतर होता। यह्या बिन सईद ने कहा के मैंने ये हदीस एक मजलिस में बयान की जिसमें यज़ीद बिन उनाइम बिन हज़्ज़ाल असलामी बैठे थे, तो यज़ीद ने कहा: हज़्ज़ाल मेरे दादा थे और ये हदीस सच है।</p>

<p dir="auto">[Muwatta Malik · 1502]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1502" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Muwatta Malik</a> · Translation origin: Human</p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="ur">
<h3>Urdu</h3>

<p dir="rtl">سعید بن مسیب سے روایت ہے کہ رسول اللہ صلی اللہ علیہ وآلہ وسلم نے کہا ایک شخص کو جو اسلم کے قبیلے سے تھا اس کا نام ہزال تھا کہ اے ہزال اگر اس خبر کو تو چھپا لیتا تو تیرے واسطے بہتر ہوتا یحیی بن سعید نے کہا کہ میں نے اس حدیث کو ایک مجلس میں بیان کیا جس میں یزید بن عنیم بن ہزال اسلمی بیٹھے تھے تو یزید نے کہا کہ ہزال میرے دادا تھے اور یہ حدیث سچ ہے ۔</p>

<p dir="rtl">[Muwatta Malik · 1502]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1502" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Muwatta Malik</a> · Translation origin: Human</p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="ur-Latn">
<h3>Roman Urdu</h3>

<p dir="ltr">Saeed bin Musayyab se riwayat hai ke Rasool Allah (Sallallahu Alaihi Wasallam) ne us shakhs (jo qabeela Aslam se tha aur uska naam Hazzal tha) se kaha: Aye Hazzal, agar tu is khabar ko chupa leta toh tere liye behtar hota. Yahya bin Saeed ne kaha ke maine ye hadith ek majlis mein bayan ki jisme Yazeed bin Unaim bin Hazzal Aslami baithe the, toh Yazeed ne kaha: Hazzal mere dada the aur ye hadith sach hai.</p>

<p dir="ltr">[Muwatta Malik · 1502]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1502" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Muwatta Malik</a> · Translation origin: Human</p>

## Narration 493

[Open this narration online](https://blaghtayt-alt.github.io/Hadith-Bank/?occurrence=deenhindi-malik%3A1505)

<p dir="auto"><strong>Chapter 41</strong></p>

<p><strong>Hadith</strong> · A narration preserved from the source.</p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="ar">
<h3>Arabic</h3>

<p dir="rtl">حَدَّثَنِي مَالِكٌ، عَنِ ابْنِ شِهَابٍ، عَنْ عُبَيْدِ اللَّهِ بْنِ عَبْدِ اللَّهِ بْنِ عُتْبَةَ بْنِ مَسْعُودٍ، عَنْ أَبِي هُرَيْرَةَ، وَزَيْدِ بْنِ خَالِدٍ الْجُهَنِيِّ، أَنَّهُمَا أَخْبَرَاهُ أَنَّ رَجُلَيْنِ اخْتَصَمَا إِلَى رَسُولِ اللَّهِ صلى الله عليه وسلم فَقَالَ أَحَدُهُمَا يَا رَسُولَ اللَّهِ اقْضِ بَيْنَنَا بِكِتَابِ اللَّهِ وَقَالَ الآخَرُ وَهُوَ أَفْقَهُهُمَا أَجَلْ يَا رَسُولَ اللَّهِ فَاقْضِ بَيْنَنَا بِكِتَابِ اللَّهِ وَائْذَنْ لِي أَنْ أَتَكَلَّمَ ‏.‏ قَالَ ‏&quot;‏ تَكَلَّمْ ‏&quot;‏ ‏.‏ فَقَالَ إِنَّ ابْنِي كَانَ عَسِيفًا عَلَى هَذَا فَزَنَا بِامْرَأَتِهِ فَأَخْبَرَنِي أَنَّ عَلَى ابْنِي الرَّجْمَ فَافْتَدَيْتُ مِنْهُ بِمِائَةِ شَاةٍ وَبِجَارِيَةٍ لِي ثُمَّ إِنِّي سَأَلْتُ أَهْلَ الْعِلْمِ فَأَخْبَرُونِي أَنَّ مَا عَلَى ابْنِي جَلْدُ مِائَةٍ وَتَغْرِيبُ عَامٍ وَأَخْبَرُونِي أَنَّمَا الرَّجْمُ عَلَى امْرَأَتِهِ ‏.‏ فَقَالَ رَسُولُ اللَّهِ ‏&quot;‏ أَمَا وَالَّذِي نَفْسِي بِيَدِهِ لأَقْضِيَنَّ بَيْنَكُمَا بِكِتَابِ اللَّهِ أَمَّا غَنَمُكَ وَجَارِيَتُكَ فَرَدٌّ عَلَيْكَ ‏&quot;‏ ‏.‏ وَجَلَدَ ابْنَهُ مِائَةً وَغَرَّبَهُ عَامًا وَأَمَرَ أُنَيْسًا الأَسْلَمِيَّ أَنْ يَأْتِيَ امْرَأَةَ الآخَرِ فَإِنِ اعْتَرَفَتْ رَجَمَهَا فَاعْتَرَفَتْ فَرَجَمَهَا ‏.‏</p>

<p dir="rtl">[Muwatta Malik · 1505]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1505" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Muwatta Malik</a></p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="en">
<h3>English</h3>

<p dir="ltr">Malik related to me from Ibn Shihab from Ubaydullah ibn Abdullah ibn Utba ibn Masud that Abu Hurayra and Zayd ibn Khalid al-Juhani informed him that two men brought a dispute to the Messenger of Allah, may Allah bless him and grant him peace. One of them said, &quot;Messenger of Allah! Judge between us by the Book of Allah!&quot; The other said, and he was the wiser of the two, &quot;Yes, Messenger of Allah. Judge between us by the Book of Allah and give me permission to speak.&quot; He said, &quot;Speak.&quot; He said, &quot;My son was hired by this person and he committed fornication with his wife. He told me that my son deserved stoning, and I ransomed him for one hundred sheep and a slave-girl. Then I asked the people of knowledge and they told me that my son deserved to be flogged with one hundred lashes and exiled for a year, and they informed me that the woman deserved to be stoned.&quot; The Messenger of Allah, may Allah bless him and grant him peace, said, &quot;By him in whose Hand myself is, I will judge between you by the Book of Allah. As for your sheep and slave girl, they should be returned to you. Your son should have one hundred lashes and be exiled for a year.&quot; He ordered Unays al-Aslami to go to the wife of the other man and to stone her if she confessed . She confessed and he stoned her</p>

<p dir="ltr">[Muwatta Malik · 1505]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1505" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Muwatta Malik</a> · Translation origin: Human</p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="hi">
<h3>Hindi</h3>

<p dir="auto">अबू हुरैरा और ज़ैद बिन ख़ालिद जुहानी से रिवायत है के दो शख़्सों ने रसूल अल्लाह (सल्लल्लाहु अलैहि वसल्लम) के पास झगड़ा किया। एक बोला: या रसूल अल्लाह, आप हमारा फ़ैसला किताबुल्लाह के मुताबिक़ कीजिये। दूसरा शख़्स जो ज़्यादा समझदार था, बोला: हाँ या रसूल अल्लाह, फ़ैसला किताबुल्लाह से कीजिये और मुझे बात करने की इजाज़त दीजिये। आप (सल्लल्लाहु अलैहि वसल्लम) ने फ़रमाया: अच्छा बोलो। उसने कहा: मेरा बेटा इस शख़्स के यहाँ नौकर था, उसने इसकी बीवी से ज़िना किया। लोगों ने मुझसे कहा के तेरे बेटे पर रजम है। मैंने 100 बकरियाँ और एक लौंडी उसकी तरफ़ से फ़िद्या दी। फिर मैंने अहले-इल्म से पूछा तो उन्होंने कहा के मेरे बेटे पर 100 कोड़े हैं और एक बरस की जला-वतनी और रजम उसकी औरत पर है। रसूल अल्लाह (सल्लल्लाहु अलैहि वसल्लम) ने फ़रमाया: मैं तुम दोनों का फ़ैसला अल्लाह की किताब के मुताबिक़ करता हूँ। तेरी बकरियाँ और लौंडी वापस ले ले। फिर उसके बेटे को 100 कोड़े मारने और एक बरस जला-वतन करने का हुकुम दिया और उनैस असलामी को हुकुम दिया के दूसरे शख़्स की बीवी के पास जाओ, अगर वो ज़िना का इक़रार करे तो उसको रजम कर दो। उसने इक़रार किया और वो रजम की गयी।</p>

<p dir="auto">[Muwatta Malik · 1505]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1505" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Muwatta Malik</a> · Translation origin: Human</p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="ur">
<h3>Urdu</h3>

<p dir="rtl">ابوہریرہ اور زید بن خالد جہنی سے روایت ہے کہ دو شخصوں نے جھگڑا کیا رسول اللہ کے پاس ایک بولا یا رسول اللہ آپ فیصلہ کیجیے ہمارا موافق کتاب اللہ کے اور دوسرا شخص جو زیادہ سمجھدار تھا وہ بولا ہاں یا رسول اللہ فیصلہ کیجیے کتاب اللہ کے اور اجازت دیجیے مجھے بات کرنے کی آپ نے فرمایا اچھا بولو اس نے کہا میرا بیٹا اس شخص کے ہاں نوکر تھا اس نے اس کی بیوی سے زنا کیا لوگوں نے مجھ سے کہا کہ تیرے بیٹے پر رجم ہے میں نے سوبکریاں اس کی طرف سے فدیہ دیں اور ایک لونڈی دی پھر میں نے اہل علم سے پوچھا تو انہوں نے کہا کہ میرے بیٹے پر سو کوڑے ہیں اور ایک برس جلاوطنی اور رجم اس کی عورت پر ہے رسول اللہ نے فرمایا تم دنوں کا فیصلہ اللہ کی کتاب کے موافق کرتا ہوں تیری بکریاں اور لونڈی تیرا مال ہے اس کولے لے اور اس کے بیٹے کو سو کوڑے مارنے کا حکم کیا اور ایک برس تک جلاوطن کیا اور حکم کیا انیس اسلمی کو کہ دوسرے شخص کی بیوی کے پاس جا اگر وہ زنا کا اقرار کرے تو اس کو رجم کر اس نے زنا کا اقرار کیا وہ رجم کی گئی۔</p>

<p dir="rtl">[Muwatta Malik · 1505]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1505" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Muwatta Malik</a> · Translation origin: Human</p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="ur-Latn">
<h3>Roman Urdu</h3>

<p dir="ltr">Abu Huraira aur Zaid bin Khalid Juhani se riwayat hai ke do shakhson ne Rasool Allah (Sallallahu Alaihi Wasallam) ke paas jhagda kiya. Ek bola: Ya Rasool Allah, aap hamara faisla kitabullah ke mutabiq kijiye. Dusra shakhs jo zyada samajhdar tha, bola: Haan ya Rasool Allah, faisla kitabullah se kijiye aur mujhe baat karne ki ijazat dijiye. Aap (Sallallahu Alaihi Wasallam) ne farmaya: Acha bolo. Usne kaha: Mera beta is shakhs ke yahan naukar tha, usne iski biwi se zina kiya. Logon ne mujhse kaha ke tere bete par rajam hai. Maine 100 bakriyan aur ek londi uski taraf se fidiya di. Phir maine ahl-e-ilm se pucha toh unhone kaha ke mere bete par 100 kode hain aur ek baras ki jala-watni aur rajam uski aurat par hai. Rasool Allah (Sallallahu Alaihi Wasallam) ne farmaya: Main tum dono ka faisla Allah ki kitab ke mutabiq karta hoon. Teri bakriyan aur londi wapas le le. Phir uske bete ko 100 kode maarne aur ek baras jala-watan karne ka hukum diya aur Unais Aslami ko hukum diya ke dusre shakhs ki biwi ke paas jao, agar wo zina ka iqrar kare toh usko rajam kar do. Usne iqrar kiya aur wo rajam ki gayi.</p>

<p dir="ltr">[Muwatta Malik · 1505]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1505" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Muwatta Malik</a> · Translation origin: Human</p>

<details class="reading-references">

## Narration 494

[Open this narration online](https://blaghtayt-alt.github.io/Hadith-Bank/?occurrence=deenhindi-malik%3A1506)

<p dir="auto"><strong>Chapter 41</strong></p>

<p><strong>Hadith</strong> · A narration preserved from the source.</p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="ar">
<h3>Arabic</h3>

<p dir="rtl">حَدَّثَنِي مَالِكٌ، عَنْ سُهَيْلِ بْنِ أَبِي صَالِحٍ، عَنْ أَبِيهِ، عَنْ أَبِي هُرَيْرَةَ، أَنَّ سَعْدَ بْنَ عُبَادَةَ، قَالَ لِرَسُولِ اللَّهِ صلى الله عليه وسلم أَرَأَيْتَ لَوْ أَنِّي وَجَدْتُ مَعَ امْرَأَتِي رَجُلاً أَأُمْهِلُهُ حَتَّى آتِيَ بِأَرْبَعَةِ شُهَدَاءَ فَقَالَ رَسُولُ اللَّهِ صلى الله عليه وسلم ‏ &quot;‏ نَعَمْ ‏&quot;‏ ‏.‏</p>

<p dir="rtl">[Muwatta Malik · 1506]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1506" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Muwatta Malik</a></p>

<details class="reading-references">
<summary>Arabic · references (1)</summary>

<p><strong>Muwatta Malik · 1506</strong><br>source site numbering not independently canonicalized</p>

<p dir="auto">[ Muwatta Malik   1506 ]</p>

</details>

<summary>Sources credited by this publisher</summary>

<p dir="auto">site level attribution not record verified</p>

<p><a href="https://sunnah.com/">Sunnah.com</a></p>

<p dir="auto">site level attribution not record verified</p>

</details>

</section>

<section class="language-reading" lang="en">
<h3>English</h3>

<p dir="ltr">Malik related to me from Suhayl ibn Abi Salih from his father from Abu Hurayra that Sad ibn Ubada said to the Messenger of Allah, may Allah bless him and grant him peace, &quot;What do you think I should do if I were to find a man with my wife? Should I leave him there until I had brought four witnesses?&quot; The Messenger of Allah, may Allah bless him and grant him peace, said, &quot;Yes</p>

<p dir="ltr">[Muwatta Malik · 1506]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1506" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Muwatta Malik</a> · Translation origin: Human</p>

<details class="reading-references">
<summary>English · references (1)</summary>

<p dir="auto">[ Muwatta Malik   1506 ]</p>

</details>

<summary>Sources credited by this publisher</summary>

<p dir="auto">site level attribution not record verified</p>

<p><a href="https://sunnah.com/">Sunnah.com</a></p>

<p dir="auto">site level attribution not record verified</p>

</details>

</section>

<section class="language-reading" lang="hi">
<h3>Hindi</h3>

<p dir="auto">अबू हुरैरा (रदी अल्लाहू अन्हु) से रिवायत है के सअद बिन उबादा ने रसूल अल्लाह (सल्लल्लाहु अलैहि वसल्लम) से कहा: अगर मैं अपनी औरत के साथ किसी मर्द को पाऊं, तो क्या मैं उसको चार गवाह जमा करने तक मोहलत दूँ? आप (सल्लल्लाहु अलैहि वसल्लम) ने फ़रमाया: हाँ।</p>

<p dir="auto">[Muwatta Malik · 1506]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1506" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Muwatta Malik</a> · Translation origin: Human</p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="ur">
<h3>Urdu</h3>

<p dir="rtl">ابوہریرہ رضی اللہ تعالیٰ عنہ سے روایت ہے کہ سعد بن عبادہ نے رسول اللہ صلی اللہ علیہ وآلہ وسلم سے کہا کہ اگر میں اپنی عورت کے ساتھ کسی مرد کو پاؤں تو کیا میں اس کو مہلت دوں چار گواہ جمع کرنے تک آپ صلی اللہ علیہ وآلہ وسلم نے فرمایا ہاں ۔</p>

<p dir="rtl">[Muwatta Malik · 1506]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1506" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Muwatta Malik</a> · Translation origin: Human</p>

## Narration 495

[Open this narration online](https://blaghtayt-alt.github.io/Hadith-Bank/?occurrence=deenhindi-malik%3A1511)

<p dir="auto"><strong>Chapter 41</strong></p>

<p><strong>Hadith</strong> · A narration preserved from the source.</p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="ar">
<h3>Arabic</h3>

<p dir="rtl">حَدَّثَنِي مَالِكٌ، عَنْ زَيْدِ بْنِ أَسْلَمَ، أَنَّ رَجُلاً، اعْتَرَفَ عَلَى نَفْسِهِ بِالزِّنَا عَلَى عَهْدِ رَسُولِ اللَّهِ صلى الله عليه وسلم فَدَعَا لَهُ رَسُولُ اللَّهِ صلى الله عليه وسلم بِسَوْطٍ فَأُتِيَ بِسَوْطٍ مَكْسُورٍ فَقَالَ ‏&quot;‏ فَوْقَ هَذَا ‏&quot;‏ ‏.‏ فَأُتِيَ بِسَوْطٍ جَدِيدٍ لَمْ تُقْطَعْ ثَمَرَتُهُ فَقَالَ ‏&quot;‏ دُونَ هَذَا ‏&quot;‏ ‏.‏ فَأُتِيَ بِسَوْطٍ قَدْ رُكِبَ بِهِ وَلاَنَ فَأَمَرَ بِهِ رَسُولُ اللَّهِ صلى الله عليه وسلم فَجُلِدَ ثُمَّ قَالَ ‏&quot;‏ أَيُّهَا النَّاسُ قَدْ آنَ لَكُمْ أَنْ تَنْتَهُوا عَنْ حُدُودِ اللَّهِ مَنْ أَصَابَ مِنْ هَذِهِ الْقَاذُورَاتِ شَيْئًا فَلْيَسْتَتِرْ بِسِتْرِ اللَّهِ فَإِنَّهُ مَنْ يُبْدِي لَنَا صَفْحَتَهُ نُقِمْ عَلَيْهِ كِتَابَ اللَّهِ ‏&quot;‏ ‏.‏</p>

<p dir="rtl">[Muwatta Malik · 1511]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1511" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Muwatta Malik</a></p>

<details class="reading-references">
<summary>Arabic · references (1)</summary>

<p><strong>Muwatta Malik · 1511</strong><br>source site numbering not independently canonicalized</p>

<p dir="auto">[ Muwatta Malik   1511 ]</p>

</details>

<details>
<summary>Sources credited by this publisher</summary>

<p dir="auto">site level attribution not record verified</p>

<p><a href="https://sunnah.com/">Sunnah.com</a></p>

<p dir="auto">site level attribution not record verified</p>

</details>

</section>

<section class="language-reading" lang="en">
<h3>English</h3>

<p dir="ltr">Malik related to me from Zayd ibn Aslam that a man confessed to fornication in the time of the Messenger of Allah, may Allah bless him and grant him peace. The Messenger of Allah, may Allah bless him and grant him peace, called for a whip, and he was brought a broken whip. He said, &quot;Above this,&quot; and he was brought a new whip whose knots had not been cut yet. He said, &quot;Below this,&quot; and he was brought a whip which had been used and made flexible. The Messenger of Allah, may Allah bless him and grant him peace, gave the order and he was flogged. Then he said, &quot;People! The time has come for you to observe the limits of Allah. Whoever has had any of these ugly things befall him should cover them up with the veil of Allah. Whoever reveals to us his wrong action, we perform what is in the Book of Allah against him</p>

<p dir="ltr">[Muwatta Malik · 1511]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1511" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Muwatta Malik</a> · Translation origin: Human</p>

<details class="reading-references">
<summary>English · references (1)</summary>

<p><strong>Muwatta Malik · 1511</strong><br>source site numbering not independently canonicalized</p>

<p dir="auto">[ Muwatta Malik   1511 ]</p>

</details>

<details>
<summary>Sources credited by this publisher</summary>

<p><a href="https://github.com/fawazahmed0/hadith-api">Fawaz Ahmed Hadith API</a></p>

<p dir="auto">site level attribution not record verified</p>

<p><a href="https://sunnah.com/">Sunnah.com</a></p>

<p dir="auto">site level attribution not record verified</p>

</details>

</section>

<section class="language-reading" lang="hi">
<h3>Hindi</h3>

<p dir="auto">ज़ैद बिन असलाम से रिवायत है के एक शख्स ने रसूल अल्लाह (सल्लल्लाहु अलैहि वसल्लम) के ज़माने में जीने का इकरार किया. आप (सल्लल्लाहु अलैहि वसल्लम) ने कूड़ा मंगवाया (सल्लल्लाहु अलैहि वसल्लम) ने फ़रमाया इस से नरम लाओ. फिर एक कोड़ा आया जो बिल्कुल टूटा हुआ था अलैहि वसल्लम) ने फ़रमाया इस से सख्त लाओ. फिर एक कोड़ा आया जो सवारी में काम आया था और नरम हो गया था (सल्लल्लाहु अलैहि वसल्लम) ने उस कोड से मारने का हुकुम दिया. बाद उसके फ़रमाया ऐ लोगों! अब वह वक़्त आ गया है के तुम बाज़ रहो अल्लाह की हदों से. जो शख्स इस क़िस्म का कोई गुनाह करे तोह चाहिए के छुपा रहे अल्लाह के परदे में जो कोई खोल देगा अपने परदे को तोह हम मुवाफ़िक़ किताब-इ-अल्लाह के उस पर हद क़ायम करेंगे.</p>

<p dir="auto">[Muwatta Malik · 1511]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1511" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Muwatta Malik</a> · Translation origin: Human</p>

</section>

<section class="language-reading" lang="ur">
<h3>Urdu</h3>

<p dir="rtl">زید بن اسلم سے روایت ہے کہ ایک شخص نے اقرار کیا زنا کا رسول اللہ صلی اللہ علیہ وآلہ وسلم کے زمانے میں آپ نے کوڑا منگوایا تو نیا کوڑا آیا جس کا سرا بھی نہیں کٹا تھا آپ صلی اللہ علیہ وآلہ وسلم نے فرمایا اس سے نرم لاؤ پھر ایک کوڑا آیا جو بالکل ٹوٹا ہوا تھا آپ صلی اللہ علیہ وآلہ وسلم نے فرما اس سے سخت لاؤ پھر ایک کوڑا آیا جو سواری میں کام آیا تھا اور نرم ہو گیا تھا آپ صلی اللہ علیہ وآلہ وسلم نے حکم کیا اس کوڑے سے مارنے کا بعد اس کے فرمایا اے لوگوں اب وہ وقت آگیا ہے کہ تم باز رہو اللہ کی حدوں سے جو شخص اس قسم کا کوئی گناہ کرے تو چاہیے کہ چھپا رہے اللہ کے پردے میں اور جو کوئی کھول دے گا اپنے پردے کو تو ہم موافق کتاب اللہ کے اس پر حد قائم کریں گے ۔</p>

<p dir="rtl">[Muwatta Malik · 1511]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1511" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Muwatta Malik</a> · Translation origin: Human</p>

<details class="reading-references">
<summary>Urdu · references (1)</summary>

<p><strong>Muwatta Malik · 1511</strong><br>source site numbering not independently canonicalized</p>

<p dir="auto">[ Muwatta Malik   1511 ]</p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="ur-Latn">
<h3>Roman Urdu</h3>

<p dir="ltr">Zaid bin Aslam se riwayat hai ke ek shakhs ne Rasool Allah (Sallallahu Alaihi Wasallam) ke zamane mein zina ka iqrar kiya. Aap (Sallallahu Alaihi Wasallam) ne koda mangwaya, toh naya koda aaya jiska sira bhi nahi kata tha. Aap (Sallallahu Alaihi Wasallam) ne farmaya is se naram lao. Phir ek koda aaya jo bilkul tota hua tha, Aap (Sallallahu Alaihi Wasallam) ne farmaya is se sakht lao. Phir ek koda aaya jo sawari mein kaam aaya tha aur naram ho gaya tha, Aap (Sallallahu Alaihi Wasallam) ne us kode se marne ka hukum diya. Baad uske farmaya ae logon! Ab woh waqt aa gaya hai ke tum baaz raho Allah ki hadon se. Jo shakhs is qism ka koi gunah kare toh chahiye ke chupa rahe Allah ke parde mein, aur jo koi khol dega apne parde ko toh hum muwafiq kitab-e-Allah ke us par had qaim karenge.</p>

<p dir="ltr">[Muwatta Malik · 1511]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1511" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Muwatta Malik</a> · Translation origin: Human</p>

## Narration 496

[Open this narration online](https://blaghtayt-alt.github.io/Hadith-Bank/?occurrence=deenhindi-malik%3A1513)

<p dir="auto"><strong>Chapter 41</strong></p>

<p><strong>Hadith</strong> · A narration preserved from the source.</p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="ar">
<h3>Arabic</h3>

<p dir="rtl">حَدَّثَنِي مَالِكٌ، عَنِ ابْنِ شِهَابٍ، عَنْ عُبَيْدِ اللَّهِ بْنِ عَبْدِ اللَّهِ بْنِ عُتْبَةَ بْنِ مَسْعُودٍ، عَنْ أَبِي هُرَيْرَةَ، وَزَيْدِ بْنِ خَالِدٍ الْجُهَنِيِّ، أَنَّ رَسُولَ اللَّهِ صلى الله عليه وسلم سُئِلَ عَنِ الأَمَةِ إِذَا زَنَتْ وَلَمْ تُحْصِنْ فَقَالَ ‏ &quot;‏ إِنْ زَنَتْ فَاجْلِدُوهَا ثُمَّ إِنْ زَنَتْ فَاجْلِدُوهَا ثُمَّ إِنْ زَنَتْ فَاجْلِدُوهَا ثُمَّ بِيعُوهَا وَلَوْ بِضَفِيرٍ ‏&quot;‏ ‏.‏ قَالَ ابْنُ شِهَابٍ لاَ أَدْرِي أَبَعْدَ الثَّالِثَةِ أَوِ الرَّابِعَةِ ‏.‏</p>

<p dir="rtl">[Muwatta Malik · 1513]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1513" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Muwatta Malik</a></p>

<details class="reading-references">
<summary>Arabic · references (1)</summary>

<p><strong>Muwatta Malik · 1513</strong><br>source site numbering not independently canonicalized</p>

<p dir="auto">[ Muwatta Malik   1513 ]</p>

</details>

<details>
<summary>Sources credited by this publisher</summary>

<p><a href="https://github.com/fawazahmed0/hadith-api">Fawaz Ahmed Hadith API</a></p>

<p dir="auto">site level attribution not record verified</p>

<p><a href="https://sunnah.com/">Sunnah.com</a></p>

<p dir="auto">site level attribution not record verified</p>

</details>

</section>

<section class="language-reading" lang="en">
<h3>English</h3>

<p dir="ltr">Malik related to me from Ibn Shihab from Ubaydullah ibn Abdullah ibn Utba ibn Masud from Abu Hurayra and Zayd ibn Khalid al-Juhani that the Messenger of Allah, may Allah bless him and grant him peace, was asked about a slave-girl who committed fornication and was not muhsana. He said, &quot;If she commits fornication, then flog her. If she commits fornication again, then flog her, and if she commits fornication again, then sell her, if only for a rope.&quot; Ibn Shihab added, &quot;I don&#x27;t know whether it was three or four times</p>

<p dir="ltr">[Muwatta Malik · 1513]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1513" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Muwatta Malik</a> · Translation origin: Human</p>

<summary>English · references (1)</summary>

<p><strong>Muwatta Malik · 1513</strong><br>source site numbering not independently canonicalized</p>

<p dir="auto">[ Muwatta Malik   1513 ]</p>

</details>

<p dir="auto">site level attribution not record verified</p>

<p><a href="https://sunnah.com/">Sunnah.com</a></p>

<p dir="auto">site level attribution not record verified</p>

</details>

</section>

<section class="language-reading" lang="hi">
<h3>Hindi</h3>

<p dir="auto">अबू हुरैरह (रज़ियल्लाहु अन्हु) और ज़ैद बिन खालिद जुहनी से रिवायत है के रसूल अल्लाह (सल्लल्लाहु अलैहि वसल्लम) से किसी ने पूछा के लोंडी ग़ैर-मुहसना जब ज़िना करे तोह क्या हुकुम है? आप (सल्लल्लाहु अलैहि वसल्लम) ने फ़रमाया के अगर वह ज़िना करे तोह उसको कोड़े मार कोड़े मारो. बाद उसके चौथी मर्तबा या तीसरी मरतबा के बाद आप (सल्लल्लाहु अलैहि वसल्लम) ने फ़रमाया बेच डालो ऐसी लोंडी को अगरचे एक रस्सी के एवज में ही हो.</p>

<p dir="auto">[Muwatta Malik · 1513]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1513" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Muwatta Malik</a> · Translation origin: Human</p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="ur">
<h3>Urdu</h3>

<p dir="rtl">ابوہریرہ رضی اللہ تعالیٰ عنہ اور زید بن خالد جہنی سے روایت ہے کہ رسول اللہ صلی اللہ علیہ وآلہ وسلم سے کسی نے پوچھا کہ لونڈی غیر محصنہ جب زنا کرے تو کیا حکم ہے آپ صلی اللہ علیہ وآلہ وسلم نے فرمایا کہ اگر وہ زنا کرے تو اس کو کوڑے مار پھر اگر زنا کرے تو پھر اس کو کوڑے مارو پھر اگر زنا کرے تو پھر اس کو کوڑے مارو بعد اس کے چوتھی مرتبہ یا تیسری مرتبہ کے بعد آپ صلی اللہ علیہ وآلہ وسلم نے فرمایا بیچ ڈالو ایسی لونڈی کو اگرچہ ایک رسی کے عوض میں ہو۔</p>

<p dir="rtl">[Muwatta Malik · 1513]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1513" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Muwatta Malik</a> · Translation origin: Human</p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="ur-Latn">
<h3>Roman Urdu</h3>

<p dir="ltr">Abu Hurairah (Radi Allahu Anhu) aur Zaid bin Khalid Juhani se riwayat hai ke Rasool Allah (Sallallahu Alaihi Wasallam) se kisi ne pucha ke londi ghair-muhsana jab zina kare toh kya hukum hai? Aap (Sallallahu Alaihi Wasallam) ne farmaya ke agar woh zina kare toh usko kode maar, phir agar zina kare toh phir usko kode maro, phir agar zina kare toh phir usko kode maro. Baad uske chauthi martaba ya teesri martaba ke baad Aap (Sallallahu Alaihi Wasallam) ne farmaya bech daalo aisi londi ko agarche ek rassi ke ewaz mein hi ho.</p>

<p dir="ltr">[Muwatta Malik · 1513]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1513" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Muwatta Malik</a> · Translation origin: Human</p>

## Narration 497

[Open this narration online](https://blaghtayt-alt.github.io/Hadith-Bank/?occurrence=deenhindi-malik%3A1521)

<p dir="auto"><strong>Chapter 41</strong></p>

<p><strong>Hadith</strong> · A narration preserved from the source.</p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="ar">
<h3>Arabic</h3>

<p dir="rtl">حَدَّثَنِي مَالِكٌ، عَنْ نَافِعٍ، عَنْ عَبْدِ اللَّهِ بْنِ عُمَرَ، أَنَّ رَسُولَ اللَّهِ صلى الله عليه وسلم قَطَعَ فِي مِجَنٍّ ثَمَنُهُ ثَلاَثَةُ دَرَاهِمَ ‏.‏</p>

<p dir="rtl">[Muwatta Malik · 1521]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1521" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Muwatta Malik</a></p>

<h3>English</h3>

<p dir="ltr">Malik related to me from Nafi from Abdullah ibn Umar that the Messenger of Allah, may Allah bless him and grant him peace, cut off the hand of a man who stole a shield whose price was three dirhams</p>

<p dir="ltr">[Muwatta Malik · 1521]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1521" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Muwatta Malik</a> · Translation origin: Human</p>

<p dir="auto">[Muwatta Malik · 1521]</p>

## Narration 498

[Open this narration online](https://blaghtayt-alt.github.io/Hadith-Bank/?occurrence=deenhindi-malik%3A1522)

<p dir="auto"><strong>Chapter 41</strong></p>

<p><strong>Hadith</strong> · A narration preserved from the source.</p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="ar">
<h3>Arabic</h3>

<p dir="rtl">وَحَدَّثَنِي عَنْ مَالِكٍ، عَنْ عَبْدِ اللَّهِ بْنِ عَبْدِ الرَّحْمَنِ بْنِ أَبِي حُسَيْنٍ الْمَكِّيِّ، أَنَّ رَسُولَ اللَّهِ صلى الله عليه وسلم قَالَ ‏ &quot;‏ لاَ قَطْعَ فِي ثَمَرٍ مُعَلَّقٍ وَلاَ فِي حَرِيسَةِ جَبَلٍ ‏&quot;‏ فَإِذَا آوَاهُ الْمُرَاحُ أَوِ الْجَرِينُ فَالْقَطْعُ فِيمَا يَبْلُغُ ثَمَنَ الْمِجَنِّ ‏.‏</p>

<p dir="rtl">[Muwatta Malik · 1522]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1522" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Muwatta Malik</a></p>

<p dir="auto">site level attribution not record verified</p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="en">
<h3>English</h3>

<p dir="ltr">Yahya related to me from Malik from Abdullah ibn Abd ar-Rahman abu Husayn al-Makki that the Messenger of Allah, may Allah bless him and grant him peace, said, &quot;The hand is not cut off for fruit hanging on the tree and for sheep kept in the mountains. So when they are taken from the fold or the place where the fruit is dried, a hand is cut off for whatever reaches the price of a shield</p>

<p dir="ltr">[Muwatta Malik · 1522]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1522" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Muwatta Malik</a> · Translation origin: Human</p>

<p dir="auto">site level attribution not record verified</p>

</details>

</section>

<section class="language-reading" lang="hi">
<h3>Hindi</h3>

<p dir="auto">अब्दुल्लाह बिन अब्दुल रहमान मक्की से रिवायत है के रसूल अल्लाह (सल्लल्लाहु अलैहि वसल्लम) ने फ़रमाया कि जो मेवा दरख़्त पर लटकता हो या जो बकरी पहाड़ पर फिरती हो उसके उठा लेने में हाथ न काटा जायेगा मगर जब वह बकरी अपने घर में आ जाये या मेवा काट कर खाने को कहीं रखा जाये फिर उसको कोई चुराये तोह हाथ काटा जायेगा बशर्त-इ-के क़ीमत उसकी ढाल के बराबर हो.</p>

<p dir="auto">[Muwatta Malik · 1522]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1522" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Muwatta Malik</a> · Translation origin: Human</p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="ur">
<h3>Urdu</h3>

<p dir="rtl">عبداللہ بن عبدالرحمن مکی سے روایت ہے کہ رسول اللہ صلی اللہ علیہ وآلہ وسلم نے فرمایا کہ جو میوہ درخت پر لٹکتا ہو یا جو بکری پہاڑ پر پھرتی ہو اس کے اٹھا لینے میں ہاتھ نہ کاٹا جائے گا مگر جب وہ بکری اپنے گھر میں آجائے یا میوہ کاٹ کر کھانے کو کہیں رکھا جائے پھر اس کو کوئی چرائے تو ہاتھ کاٹا جائے گا بشرطیکہ قیمت اس کی ڈھال کے برابر ہو ۔</p>

<p dir="rtl">[Muwatta Malik · 1522]</p>

## Narration 499

[Open this narration online](https://blaghtayt-alt.github.io/Hadith-Bank/?occurrence=deenhindi-malik%3A1529)

<p dir="auto"><strong>Chapter 41</strong></p>

<p><strong>Hadith</strong> · A narration preserved from the source.</p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="ar">
<h3>Arabic</h3>

<p dir="rtl">وَحَدَّثَنِي عَنْ مَالِكٍ، عَنِ ابْنِ شِهَابٍ، عَنْ صَفْوَانَ بْنِ عَبْدِ اللَّهِ بْنِ صَفْوَانَ، أَنَّ صَفْوَانَ بْنَ أُمَيَّةَ، قِيلَ لَهُ إِنَّهُ مَنْ لَمْ يُهَاجِرْ هَلَكَ ‏.‏ فَقَدِمَ صَفْوَانُ بْنُ أُمَيَّةَ الْمَدِينَةَ فَنَامَ فِي الْمَسْجِدِ وَتَوَسَّدَ رِدَاءَهُ فَجَاءَ سَارِقٌ فَأَخَذَ رِدَاءَهُ فَأَخَذَ صَفْوَانُ السَّارِقَ فَجَاءَ بِهِ إِلَى رَسُولِ اللَّهِ صلى الله عليه وسلم فَقَالَ لَهُ رَسُولُ اللَّهِ صلى الله عليه وسلم ‏&quot;‏ أَسَرَقْتَ رِدَاءَ هَذَا ‏&quot;‏ ‏.‏ قَالَ نَعَمْ ‏.‏ فَأَمَرَ بِهِ رَسُولُ اللَّهِ صلى الله عليه وسلم أَنْ تُقْطَعَ يَدُهُ فَقَالَ لَهُ صَفْوَانُ إِنِّي لَمْ أُرِدْ هَذَا يَا رَسُولَ اللَّهِ هُوَ عَلَيْهِ صَدَقَةٌ ‏.‏ فَقَالَ رَسُولُ اللَّهِ صلى الله عليه وسلم ‏&quot;‏ فَهَلاَّ قَبْلَ أَنْ تَأْتِيَنِي بِهِ ‏&quot;‏ ‏.‏</p>

<p dir="rtl">[Muwatta Malik · 1529]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1529" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Muwatta Malik</a></p>

<details class="reading-references">
<summary>Arabic · references (1)</summary>

<p><strong>Muwatta Malik · 1529</strong><br>source site numbering not independently canonicalized</p>

<p dir="auto">[ Muwatta Malik   1529 ]</p>

</details>

<p dir="auto">site level attribution not record verified</p>

</section>

<section class="language-reading" lang="en">
<h3>English</h3>

<p dir="ltr">Yahya related to me from Malik from Ibn Shihab from Safwan ibn Abdullah ibn Safwan that it was said to Safwan ibn Umayya, &quot;Whoever does not do hijra is ruined.&quot; So Safwan ibn Umayya went to Madina and slept in the mosque with his cloak as a pillow. A thief came and took his cloak and Safwan grabbed hold of the thief and brought him to the Messenger of Allah, may Allah bless him and grant him peace. The Messenger of Allah, may Allah bless him and grant him peace, said to him, &quot;Did you steal this cloak?&quot; He said, &quot;Yes.&quot; So the Messenger of Allah, may Allah bless him and grant him peace, ordered that his hand be cut off. Safwan said to him, &quot;I did not intend this. It is his as sadaqa.&quot; The Messenger of Allah, may Allah bless him and grant him peace, said, &quot;Why didn&#x27;t you do it before bringing him to me?</p>

<p dir="ltr">[Muwatta Malik · 1529]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1529" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Muwatta Malik</a> · Translation origin: Human</p>

<details class="reading-references">
<summary>English · references (1)</summary>

<p><strong>Muwatta Malik · 1529</strong><br>source site numbering not independently canonicalized</p>

<p dir="auto">[ Muwatta Malik   1529 ]</p>

</details>

<p dir="auto">site level attribution not record verified</p>

<p><a href="https://sunnah.com/">Sunnah.com</a></p>

<p dir="auto">site level attribution not record verified</p>

</details>

</section>

<section class="language-reading" lang="hi">
<h3>Hindi</h3>

<p dir="auto">सफ़वान बिन अब्दुल्लाह बिन सफ़वान से रिवायत है के सफ़वान बिन उमय्या से किसी ने कहा के जिसने हिजरत नहीं की तो वो तबाह हुआ, तो सफ़वान मदीना में आये और मस्जिद-ए-नबवी में अपनी चादर सर के तले रख कर सो रहे। चोर आया और चादर उनकी ले गया। सफ़वान ने उठ कर चोर को गिरफ़्तार किया और रसूल अल्लाह (सल्लल्लाहु अलैहि वसल्लम) के पास ले आये। आपने चोर से पूछा के क्या तू ने सफ़वान की चादर चुरायी? वो बोला हाँ। आपने उसका हाथ काटने का हुक्म किया। सफ़वान ने कहा मेरी नियत यह न थी या रसूल अल्लाह, वो चादर तो इस पर सदक़ा है। आपने फ़रमाया तुझको यह अमर मेरे पास लाने से पहले करना था।</p>

<p dir="auto">[Muwatta Malik · 1529]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1529" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Muwatta Malik</a> · Translation origin: Human</p>

</section>

<section class="language-reading" lang="ur">
<h3>Urdu</h3>

<p dir="rtl">صفوان بن عبداللہ بن صفوان سے روایت ہے کہ صفوان بن امیہ سے کسی نے کہا کہ جس نے ہجرت نہیں کی تو وہ تباہ ہوا تو صفوان مدینہ میں آئے اور مسجد نبوی میں اپنی چادر سر کے تلے رکھ کر سو رہے چور آیا اور چادر ان کی لے گیا صفوان نے اٹھ کر چور کو گرفتار کیا اور رسول اللہ کے پاس لے آئے آپ نے چور سے پوچھا کہ کیا تو نے صفوان کی چادر چرائی وہ بولا ہاں آپ نے اس کا ہاتھ کاٹنے کا حکم کیا صفوان نے کہا میری نیت یہ نہ تھی یا رسول اللہ وہ چادر تو اس پر صدقہ ہے آپ نے فرمایا تجھ کو یہ امر میرے پاس لانے سے پہلے کرنا تھا</p>

<p dir="rtl">[Muwatta Malik · 1529]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1529" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Muwatta Malik</a> · Translation origin: Human</p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="ur-Latn">
<h3>Roman Urdu</h3>

<p dir="ltr">Safwan bin Abdullah bin Safwan se riwayat hai ke Safwan bin Umayya se kisi ne kaha ke jisne hijrat nahi ki toh woh tabah hua, toh Safwan Madina mein aaye aur Masjid-e-Nabvi mein apni chadar sar ke tale rakh kar so rahe. Chor aaya aur chadar unki le gaya. Safwan ne uth kar chor ko giraftar kiya aur Rasool Allah (Sallallahu Alaihi Wasallam) ke paas le aaye. Aapne chor se poocha ke kya tu ne Safwan ki chadar churayi? Woh bola haan. Aapne uska haath kaatne ka hukm kiya. Safwan ne kaha meri niyat yeh na thi ya Rasool Allah, woh chadar toh is par sadqa hai. Aapne farmaya tujhko yeh amar mere paas laane se pehle karna tha.</p>

<p dir="ltr">[Muwatta Malik · 1529]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1529" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Muwatta Malik</a> · Translation origin: Human</p>

## Narration 500

[Open this narration online](https://blaghtayt-alt.github.io/Hadith-Bank/?occurrence=deenhindi-malik%3A1533)

<p dir="auto"><strong>Chapter 41</strong></p>

<p><strong>Hadith</strong> · A narration preserved from the source.</p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="ar">
<h3>Arabic</h3>

<p dir="rtl">وَحَدَّثَنِي يَحْيَى، عَنْ مَالِكٍ، عَنْ يَحْيَى بْنِ سَعِيدٍ، عَنْ مُحَمَّدِ بْنِ يَحْيَى بْنِ حَبَّانَ، أَنَّ عَبْدًا، سَرَقَ وَدِيًّا مِنْ حَائِطِ رَجُلٍ فَغَرَسَهُ فِي حَائِطِ سَيِّدِهِ فَخَرَجَ صَاحِبُ الْوَدِيِّ يَلْتَمِسُ وَدِيَّهُ فَوَجَدَهُ فَاسْتَعْدَى عَلَى الْعَبْدِ مَرْوَانَ بْنَ الْحَكَمِ فَسَجَنَ مَرْوَانُ الْعَبْدَ وَأَرَادَ قَطْعَ يَدِهِ فَانْطَلَقَ سَيِّدُ الْعَبْدِ إِلَى رَافِعِ بْنِ خَدِيجٍ فَسَأَلَهُ عَنْ ذَلِكَ فَأَخْبَرَهُ أَنَّهُ سَمِعَ رَسُولَ اللَّهِ صلى الله عليه وسلم يَقُولُ ‏&quot;‏ لاَ قَطْعَ فِي ثَمَرٍ وَلاَ كَثَرٍ ‏&quot;‏ ‏.‏ وَالْكَثَرُ الْجُمَّارُ ‏.‏ فَقَالَ الرَّجُلُ فَإِنَّ مَرْوَانَ بْنَ الْحَكَمِ أَخَذَ غُلاَمًا لِي وَهُوَ يُرِيدُ قَطْعَهُ وَأَنَا أُحِبُّ أَنْ تَمْشِيَ مَعِيَ إِلَيْهِ فَتُخْبِرَهُ بِالَّذِي سَمِعْتَ مِنْ رَسُولِ اللَّهِ صلى الله عليه وسلم ‏.‏ فَمَشَى مَعَهُ رَافِعٌ إِلَى مَرْوَانَ بْنِ الْحَكَمِ فَقَالَ أَخَذْتَ غُلاَمًا لِهَذَا فَقَالَ نَعَمْ ‏.‏ فَقَالَ فَمَا أَنْتَ صَانِعٌ بِهِ قَالَ أَرَدْتُ قَطْعَ يَدِهِ ‏.‏ فَقَالَ لَهُ رَافِعٌ سَمِعْتُ رَسُولَ اللَّهِ صلى الله عليه وسلم يَقُولُ ‏&quot;‏ لاَ قَطْعَ فِي ثَمَرٍ وَلاَ كَثَرٍ ‏&quot;‏ ‏.‏ فَأَمَرَ مَرْوَانُ بِالْعَبْدِ فَأُرْسِلَ ‏.‏</p>

<p dir="rtl">[Muwatta Malik · 1533]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1533" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Muwatta Malik</a></p>
